# PacEdu Form 3 English — 460 Daily Printable Worksheets
**Status:** PacEdu draft only. PENDING_TEACHER_REVIEW; PENDING_OFFICIAL_INDICATOR_VERIFICATION. Not a Ministry-issued pack.
115 days × four separate Copper/Bronze/Silver/Gold worksheets. Use the dedicated viewer to select one day and pathway. School calendar controls teaching days; school IANA timezone supplies local print date/time, with a visible warning if not configured.
Learner: __________ School: __________ Teacher: __________ Level: Form 3
Accessibility: accept speech, sign, AAC, pointing, gesture, dictation or writing as appropriate. Credit equivalent vocabulary and supported interpretations. Do not penalise accent or disability-related speech differences.

---

## Day 1 · Term 1 Week 1 Day 1 · Copper
**Theme:** Identity, voice and community | **Strand:** Listening and speaking
**Objective:** Explore narrative voice, audience and context; close-read a text; create coherent paragraphs.
**PacEdu working achievement indicator:** Learner can listen actively, contribute to discussion and ask a purposeful follow-up question.
**Task:** Listen actively, contribute to discussion and ask a purposeful follow-up question. Use today's authorised text or teacher-selected prompt. Identify the main idea, one supporting detail and one question or inference.
**Copper:** Model each step; chunk the task; use accessible short text, visual/tactile cues, a word bank and guided choices. Accept speech, sign, AAC, pointing, gesture, dictation or writing.
**Worked example:** Identify task/audience → find a relevant idea or text detail → organise response → draft → review whether evidence and language fit the purpose. Teacher models with today's text.
**Learner response:** _________________________________________________
**Answer guidance:** Credit accurate answers supported by text; accept synonyms, paraphrase and alternative interpretations when evidence is supplied. Check only taught language conventions.
**Evidence / feedback:** ______________________ **Official indicator/source page:** PENDING VERIFICATION **Review:** PENDING TEACHER REVIEW
